scripted_gui = {
	TFR_scw_open = {
		context_type = player_context
	ai_enabled = { always = no }
		window_name = "TFR_scw_open_window"
		parent_window_name = "raid_filter"
		dirty = TFR_scw_ui_dirty
		# No visible trigger: the button is always shown. When the quota panels do not apply (war over, period ended, or the
		# country takes no part), TFR_scw_info explains why instead.
		effects = {
			open_click = {
			 if = { limit = { has_country_flag = TFR_scw_panel_open } clr_country_flag = TFR_scw_panel_open }
			 else = { set_country_flag = TFR_scw_panel_open }
			 if = {
			  limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { TFR_scw_view > 0 } } }
			  set_variable = { TFR_scw_view = 2 }
			  if = { limit = { original_tag = GER } set_variable = { TFR_scw_view = 1 } }
			  if = { limit = { original_tag = SOV } set_variable = { TFR_scw_view = 3 } }
			 }
			 if = { limit = { tag = GER } TFR_scw_axis_prepare = yes }
			 if = { limit = { tag = ENG } TFR_scw_allies_prepare = yes }
			 if = { limit = { tag = SOV } TFR_scw_soviet_prepare = yes }
			 TFR_scw_sync_view = yes
			}
		}
	}
	TFR_scw_info = {
		context_type = player_context
	ai_enabled = { always = no }
		window_name = "TFR_scw_info_window"
		dirty = TFR_scw_ui_dirty
		visible = { has_country_flag = TFR_scw_panel_open NOT = { TFR_scw_can_view = yes } }
		effects = {
			close_click = { clr_country_flag = TFR_scw_panel_open add_to_variable = { TFR_scw_ui_dirty = 1 } }
		}
		triggers = {
			over_text_visible = { NOT = { TFR_scw_manager_in_period = yes } }
			none_text_visible = { TFR_scw_manager_in_period = yes }
		}
	}
	TFR_scw_axis = {
		context_type = player_context
	ai_enabled = { always = no }
		window_name = "TFR_scw_axis_window"
		dirty = TFR_scw_ui_dirty
		visible = { TFR_scw_can_view = yes has_country_flag = TFR_scw_panel_open check_variable = { TFR_scw_view = 1 } }
		effects = {
			close_click = { clr_country_flag = TFR_scw_panel_open add_to_variable = { TFR_scw_ui_dirty = 1 } }
			apply_click = { if = { limit = { original_tag = GER } TFR_scw_axis_commit = yes } }
			tab_axis_click = { if = { limit = { TFR_scw_can_view = yes } set_variable = { TFR_scw_view = 1 } TFR_scw_sync_view = yes } }
			tab_allies_click = { if = { limit = { TFR_scw_can_view = yes } set_variable = { TFR_scw_view = 2 } TFR_scw_sync_view = yes } }
			tab_soviet_click = { if = { limit = { TFR_scw_can_view = yes original_tag = SOV } set_variable = { TFR_scw_view = 3 } TFR_scw_sync_view = yes } }
			GER_land_set_0_click = {
			 if = {
			  limit = { original_tag = GER TFR_scw_manager_in_period = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = TFR_scw_month_committed } GER = { exists = yes } OR = { check_variable = { TFR_scw_GER_land_available > -1 } GER = { check_variable = { TFR_scw_draft_land > -1 } } } }
			  GER = { set_variable = { TFR_scw_draft_land = 0 } }
			  TFR_scw_axis_prepare = yes
			  TFR_scw_refresh_views = yes
			 }
			}
			GER_land_set_1_click = {
			 if = {

		context_type = player_context
	ai_enabled = { always = no }
		window_name = "TFR_scw_allies_window"
		dirty = TFR_scw_ui_dirty
		visible = { TFR_scw_can_view = yes has_country_flag = TFR_scw_panel_open check_variable = { TFR_scw_view = 2 } }
		effects = {
			close_click = { clr_country_flag = TFR_scw_panel_open add_to_variable = { TFR_scw_ui_dirty = 1 } }
			apply_click = { if = { limit = { original_tag = ENG } TFR_scw_allies_commit = yes } }
			tab_axis_click = { if = { limit = { TFR_scw_can_view = yes } set_variable = { TFR_scw_view = 1 } TFR_scw_sync_view = yes } }
